Conclusion: 语言作为潜在变量能够扩展模型的潜在推理空间，多语言训练带来一致且可泛化的推理性能提升，验证了语言对模型内部推理结构的调节作用。

Abstract: As LLMs reduce English-centric bias, a surprising trend emerges: non-English responses sometimes outperform English on reasoning tasks. We hypothesize that language functions as a latent variable that structurally modulates the model's internal inference pathways, rather than merely serving as an output medium. To test this, we conducted a Polyglot Thinking Experiment, in which models were prompted to solve identical problems under language-constrained and language-unconstrained conditions. Results show that non-English responses often achieve higher accuracy, and the best performance frequently occur when language is unconstrained, suggesting that multilinguality broadens the model's latent reasoning space. Based on this insight, we propose polyGRPO (Polyglot Group Relative Policy Optimization), an RL framework that treats language variation as an implicit exploration signal. It generates polyglot preference data online under language-constrained and unconstrained conditions, optimizing the policy with respect to both answer accuracy and reasoning structure. Trained on only 18.1K multilingual math problems without chain-of-thought annotations, polyGRPO improves the base model (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) by 6.72% absolute accuracy on four English reasoning testset and 6.89% in their multilingual benchmark. Remarkably, it is the only method that surpasses the base LLM on English commonsense reasoning task (4.9%), despite being trained solely on math data-highlighting its strong cross-task generalization. Further analysis reveals that treating language as a latent variable expands the model's latent reasoning space, yielding consistent and generalizable improvements in reasoning performance.

</details>


### [37] [Process Supervision via Verbal Critique Improves Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21611)
*Hao-Yuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出言语过程监督（VPS）框架，通过强监督者的结构化自然语言批评指导迭代生成-批评-修正循环，在GPQA Diamond、AIME 2025、LiveCodeBench V6上取得显著提升，确立批评粒度作为推理时扩展的新维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展主要关注链深度、样本广度、学习型步骤评分器三个维度，缺乏对外部语言监督粒度的系统性探索。作者希望引入第四个维度——外部语言监督的粒度，以进一步提升大语言模型的推理性能。

Method: 提出Verbal Process Supervision (VPS)框架，一种免训练方法，利用更强监督者生成结构化自然语言批评，在迭代生成-批评-修正循环中指导模型推理，最多进行R轮迭代。

Result: 1) GPQA Diamond上GPT-5.4 (High)|GPT-5.4 (Low)在R=4时达94.9%，超越此前94.1%的SOTA；2) AIME 2025上弱模型得分从11.7-26.7%提升至63.3-90.0%（最高+63.3点）；3) 同等计算量下VPS优于Reflexion（+8.5至+12.1点）和Self-Consistency@5（+5.0至+8.3点）。性能与监督者-参与者能力差距呈强正相关（Pearson r=0.90）。

Conclusion: 批评粒度可作为推理时扩展的新维度，但该方法在错误无法通过语言表达的场景（如代码合成）中性能会下降，提示需要混合语言-可执行方法。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling for LLM reasoning has focused on three axes: chain depth, sample breadth, and learned step-scorers (PRMs). We introduce a fourth axis, granularity of external verbal supervision, via Verbal Process Supervision (VPS), a training-free framework that uses structured natural-language critique from a stronger supervisor to guide an iterative generate-critique-refine loop up to a round budget R. Across GPQA Diamond, AIME 2025, and LiveCodeBench V6 (covering both closed and open models), VPS yields three key results. First, on GPQA Diamond, GPT-5.4 (High) | GPT-5.4 (Low) reaches 94.9% at R=4, surpassing the 94.1% state of the art without gradient updates. Second, on AIME 2025, VPS enables strong weak-actor rescue, boosting scores from 11.7-26.7% to 63.3-90.0% (up to +63.3 points). Third, at matched compute, VPS outperforms Reflexion by +8.5 to +12.1 points and Self-Consistency@5 by +5.0 pp (GPQA) and +8.3 pp (LiveCodeBench), isolating critique granularity as the key driver. Performance scales with the supervisor-actor capability gap (Pearson r=0.90) and degrades when errors are not linguistically expressible (e.g., code synthesis), motivating hybrid verbal-executable methods. These results establish critique granularity as a new axis of inference-time scaling.

</details>


### [38] [Multilinguality at the Edge: Developing Language Models for the Global South](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21637)
*Lester James V. Miranda,Songbo Hu,Roi Reichart,Anna Korhonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文调查了232篇相关文献，系统分析了将多语言能力与边缘部署相结合以服务全球南方非英语和硬件受限社区的"最后一英里"挑战，探讨了从数据收集到模型部署全流程的技术瓶颈，并为NLP生态系统中的不同利益相关者提供了可操作建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的部署决定了谁能从中受益。然而，全球南方的非英语和硬件受限社区面临有效部署的多重障碍，而多语言NLP与边缘部署研究长期相互割裂，亟需将两者结合起来研究。

Method: 对232篇涉及多语言与边缘部署交叉问题的论文进行了系统性文献调研，覆盖语言建模全流程（数据收集、开发到部署）。

Result: 揭示了将多语言能力与边缘计算结合面临的技术竞争性需求与挑战，识别了当前研究中的空白与瓶颈。

Conclusion: 多语言性和边缘部署的目标虽一致但技术要求常相互冲突，两者的交叉研究既是解决语言多样性社区基础设施限制的迫切需求，也是推动包容性语言技术发展的机遇，论文为NLP生态系统各方提供了行动建议。

Abstract: Where and how language models (LMs) are deployed determines who can benefit from them. However, there are several challenges that prevent effective deployment of LMs in non-English-speaking and hardware constrained communities in the Global South. We call this challenge the last mile: the intersection of multilinguality and edge deployment, where the goals are aligned but the technical requirements often compete. Studying these two fields together is both a need, as linguistically diverse communities often face the most severe infrastructure constraints, and an opportunity, as edge and multilingual NLP research remain largely siloed. To understand the state of the art and the challenges of combining the two areas, we survey 232 papers that tackle this problem across the language modelling pipeline, from data collection to development and deployment. We also discuss open questions and provide actionable recommendations for different stakeholders in the NLP ecosystem. Finally, we hope that this work contributes to the development of inclusive and equitable language technologies.

</details>


### [39] [Fine-Grained Perspectives: Modeling Explanations with Annotator-Specific Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21667)
*Olufunke O. Sarumi,Charles Welch,Daniel Braun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出联合建模标注者标签预测与解释生成的框架，通过User Passport机制融合标注者身份与人口统计信息，设计后提示解释器与前缀桥解释器两种架构。实验表明，引入解释建模能显著提升标注者感知分类器的性能，前缀桥方法在标签对齐与语义一致性上更优，后提示方法则词汇相似度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注标签层面的人群差异，但缺乏对标注者个体层面细粒度视角（如标注理由）的建模。本文旨在利用标注者提供的解释信号，将视角建模从标签粒度推进到解释粒度，更忠实地表征标注者之间的分歧。

Method: 1) 使用User Passport机制在表示层注入标注者身份与人口统计信息；2) 提出后提示解释器（post-hoc prompt-based explainer）和前缀桥解释器（prefixed bridge explainer）两种架构，将标注者条件化的分类器表示迁移到生成模型中，生成与个体视角一致的合理解释。

Result: 1) 引入解释建模显著提升标注者感知分类器的预测性能；2) 前缀桥方法在标签对齐稳定性和语义一致性方面更优；3) 后提示方法在词汇相似度方面表现更强。

Conclusion: 将解释建模作为细粒度视角的表达，能够更丰富、更忠实地表征标注者分歧。所提框架通过将标注者特定理由同时整合到预测和生成组件中，推进了视角主义建模。

Abstract: Beyond exploring disaggregated labels for modeling perspectives, annotator rationales provide fine-grained signals of individual perspectives. In this work, we propose a framework for jointly modeling annotator-specific label prediction and corresponding explanations, fine-tuned on the annotators' provided rationales. Using a dataset with disaggregated natural language inference (NLI) annotations and annotator-provided explanations, we condition predictions on both annotator identity and demographic metadata through a representation-level User Passport mechanism. We further introduce two explainer architectures: a post-hoc prompt-based explainer and a prefixed bridge explainer that transfers annotator-conditioned classifier representations directly into a generative model. This design enables explanation generation aligned with individual annotator perspectives. Our results show that incorporating explanation modeling substantially improves predictive performance over a baseline annotator-aware classifier, with the prefixed bridge approach achieving more stable label alignment and higher semantic consistency, while the post-hoc approach yields stronger lexical similarity. These findings indicate that modeling explanations as expressions of fine-grained perspective provides a richer and more faithful representation of disagreement. The proposed approaches advance perspectivist modeling by integrating annotator-specific rationales into both predictive and generative components.

</details>

*Marius Huber,David R. Reich,Lena A. Jäger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用持久同调（拓扑数据分析方法）对眼动追踪数据中的注视序列进行多尺度特征提取，通过构建结合拓扑特征与传统统计特征的混合模型，在哥本哈根语料库的阅读障碍检测任务中取得了优于纯传统方法的效果，并提出了比现有方法更优的新型过滤方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索持久同调这一拓扑数据分析方法在眼动追踪数据分析中的适用性，并利用其提取的多尺度稳健特征来改善阅读障碍检测性能。

Method: 将注视序列视为时间序列，开发新的过滤方法（filtration）用于持久同调分析；构建混合模型，融合拓扑特征与传统统计特征；在哥本哈根语料库上对L1和L2读者中的阅读障碍检测任务进行实证评估。

Conclusion: 持久同调能够捕获注视序列中与传统特征互补的信息，拓扑特征对眼动追踪分析具有重要价值，且所提新过滤方法在该领域更具优势。

Abstract: Persistent homology, a method from topological data analysis, extracts robust, multi-scale features from data. It produces stable representations of time series by applying varying thresholds to their values (a process known as a \textit{filtration}). We develop novel filtrations for time series and introduce topological methods for the analysis of eye-tracking data, by interpreting fixation sequences as time series, and constructing ``hybrid models'' that combine topological features with traditional statistical features. We empirically evaluate our method by applying it to the task of dyslexia detection from eye-tracking-while-reading data using the Copenhagen Corpus, which contains scanpaths from dyslexic and non-dyslexic L1 and L2 readers. Our hybrid models outperform existing approaches that rely solely on traditional features, showing that persistent homology captures complementary information encoded in fixation sequences. The strength of these topological features is further underscored by their achieving performance comparable to established baseline methods. Importantly, our proposed filtrations outperform existing ones.

</details>


### [41] [Beyond N-gram: Data-Aware X-GRAM Extraction for Efficient Embedding Parameter Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21724)
*Yilong Chen,Yanxi Xie,Zitian Gao,He Xin,Yihao Xiao,Renbiao Liu,Haoming Luo,Yifan Luo,Zhengmao Ye,Tingwen Liu,Xin Zhao,Ran Tao,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: X-GRAM 提出了一种频率感知的动态令牌注入框架，通过混合哈希、别名混合、归一化 SwiGLU ShortConv 和深度感知门控等技术，解决了大规模令牌索引查找表中长尾欠训练、层间异构需求和槽坍塌导致的参数效率低下和内存快速增长问题。在 0.73B 和 1.15B 规模上，相比基线提升最高 4.4 个点，且使用更小的查找表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模令牌索引查找表虽能通过计算解耦实现扩展，但存在参数效率低下和内存快速增长的问题，根源在于长尾分布的欠训练、不同层对记忆的异构需求以及"槽坍塌"导致的冗余嵌入。

Method: 提出 X-GRAM 框架：1）采用混合哈希和别名混合压缩长尾并保留头部容量；2）通过归一化 SwiGLU ShortConv 精炼检索向量以提取局部 n-gram 特征；3）利用深度感知门控将这些信号集成到注意力值流和层间残差中，实现静态记忆与动态上下文的对齐。

Result: 在 0.73B 和 1.15B 规模上，X-GRAM 相比原始骨干提升平均准确率 4.4 个点，相比强检索基线提升 3.2 个点，且在 50% 配置下使用更小的查找表。该设计实现了模型容量与 FLOPs 的解耦。

Conclusion: X-GRAM 通过高效内存管理将容量与计算解耦，为未来记忆增强型架构提供了一种可扩展且实用的范式。

Abstract: Large token-indexed lookup tables provide a compute-decoupled scaling path, but their practical gains are often limited by poor parameter efficiency and rapid memory growth. We attribute these limitations to Zipfian under-training of the long tail, heterogeneous demand across layers, and "slot collapse" that produces redundant embeddings. To address this, we propose X-GRAM, a frequency-aware dynamic token-injection framework. X-GRAM employs hybrid hashing and alias mixing to compress the tail while preserving head capacity, and refines retrieved vectors via normalized SwiGLU ShortConv to extract diverse local n-gram features. These signals are integrated into attention value streams and inter-layer residuals using depth-aware gating, effectively aligning static memory with dynamic context. This design introduces a memory-centric scaling axis that decouples model capacity from FLOPs. Extensive evaluations at the 0.73B and 1.15B scales show that X-GRAM improves average accuracy by as much as 4.4 points over the vanilla backbone and 3.2 points over strong retrieval baselines, while using substantially smaller tables in the 50% configuration. Overall, by decoupling capacity from compute through efficient memory management, X-GRAM offers a scalable and practical paradigm for future memory-augmented architectures. Code aviliable in https://github.com/Longyichen/X-gram.

</details>


### [42] [AEL: Agent Evolving Learning for Open-Ended Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21725)
*Wujiang Xu,Jiaojiao Han,Minghao Guo,Kai Mei,Xi Zhu,Han Zhang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent Evolving Learning (AEL)框架，通过双时间尺度机制（快速汤普森采样记忆检索策略 + 慢速LLM驱动反思）解决LLM智能体在开放环境中的状态持久化问题。在208个时间步的序列投资组合基准测试中，AEL实现2.13±0.47的夏普比率，优于5种自改进方法及所有非LLM基线，消融实验显示记忆+反思带来58%累积提升，但额外机制均导致性能下降，揭示瓶颈在于自我诊断如何使用经验而非增加架构复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在开放环境中运行数百个连续回合时仍然是"无状态"的——每个任务从零开始解决，无法将过往经验转化为更好的未来行为。核心障碍不在于记住什么，而在于如何使用已记住的内容，包括应用何种检索策略、如何解释先前结果、以及何时改变当前策略本身。

Method: 提出AEL双时间尺度框架：快速时间尺度使用汤普森采样bandit学习每个回合应采用的记忆检索策略；慢速时间尺度通过LLM驱动的反思诊断失败模式，将因果洞察注入智能体的决策提示中，为其检索到的证据提供解释框架。

Result: 在10个行业多样化股票、208个回合、5个随机种子的序列投资组合基准上，AEL实现夏普比率2.13±0.47，优于五种已发表的自改进方法和所有非LLM基线，且在LLM方法中方差最低。九变体消融实验显示，记忆+反思带来58%累积提升，但所有额外机制（规划器进化、工具选择、冷启动初始化、技能提取、三种信用分配方法）均导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 智能体自我改进的瓶颈在于自我诊断如何使用经验，而非增加架构复杂度，呈现"少即是多"的模式。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate in open-ended environments spanning hundreds of sequential episodes, yet they remain largely stateless: each task is solved from scratch without converting past experience into better future behavior. The central obstacle is not \emph{what} to remember but \emph{how to use} what has been remembered, including which retrieval policy to apply, how to interpret prior outcomes, and when the current strategy itself must change. We introduce \emph{Agent Evolving Learning} (\ael{}), a two-timescale framework that addresses this obstacle. At the fast timescale, a Thompson Sampling bandit learns which memory retrieval policy to apply at each episode; at the slow timescale, LLM-driven reflection diagnoses failure patterns and injects causal insights into the agent's decision prompt, giving it an interpretive frame for the evidence it retrieves. On a sequential portfolio benchmark (10 sector-diverse tickers, 208 episodes, 5 random seeds), \ael{} achieves a Sharpe ratio of 2.13$\pm$0.47, outperforming five published self-improving methods and all non-LLM baselines while maintaining the lowest variance among all LLM-based approaches. A nine-variant ablation reveals a ``less is more'' pattern: memory and reflection together produce a 58\% cumulative improvement over the stateless baseline, yet every additional mechanism we test (planner evolution, per-tool selection, cold-start initialization, skill extraction, and three credit assignment methods) \emph{degrades} performance. This demonstrates that the bottleneck in agent self-improvement is \emph{self-diagnosing how to use} experience rather than adding architectural complexity. Code and data: https://github.com/WujiangXu/AEL.

</details>


### [43] [StructMem: Structured Memory for Long-Horizon Behavior in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21748)
*Buqiang Xu,Yijun Chen,Jizhan Fang,Ruobin Zhong,Yunzhi Yao,Yuqi Zhu,Lun Du,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出StructMem，一种结构增强的分层记忆框架，通过保持事件级绑定和诱导跨事件连接，在降低token使用、API调用和运行时的同时，提升了时序推理和多跳问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期对话智能体需要能够捕捉事件间关系的记忆系统，而现有方法在扁平记忆（高效但缺乏结构）和图记忆（支持结构化推理但构建昂贵且脆弱）之间存在根本性权衡。

Method: 提出StructMem，一种结构增强的分层记忆框架，通过时序锚定双视角（temporally anchoring dual perspectives）和周期性语义整合（periodic semantic consolidation），保持事件级绑定并诱导跨事件连接。

Result: 在LoCoMo数据集上，StructMem提升了时序推理和多跳问答性能，同时相比先前记忆系统大幅减少了token使用、API调用和运行时间。

Conclusion: StructMem有效解决了记忆系统中效率与结构化推理之间的权衡问题，为长期对话智能体的记忆系统设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Long-term conversational agents need memory systems that capture relationships between events, not merely isolated facts, to support temporal reasoning and multi-hop question answering. Current approaches face a fundamental trade-off: flat memory is efficient but fails to model relational structure, while graph-based memory enables structured reasoning at the cost of expensive and fragile construction. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{StructMem}, a structure-enriched hierarchical memory framework that preserves event-level bindings and induces cross-event connections. By temporally anchoring dual perspectives and performing periodic semantic consolidation, StructMem improves temporal reasoning and multi-hop performance on \texttt{LoCoMo}, while substantially reducing token usage, API calls, and runtime compared to prior memory systems, see https://github.com/zjunlp/LightMem .

</details>


### [44] [Why are all LLMs Obsessed with Japanese Culture? On the Hidden Cultural and Regional Biases of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21751)
*Joseba Fernandez de Landa,Carla Perez-Almendros,Jose Camacho-Collados*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于文化相关开放问题（CROQ）的数据集，发现LLM在文化问题上存在区域偏好（尤其偏向日本），且这种偏见在监督微调阶段而非预训练阶段出现；高资源语言提示会带来更多样化输出并减少对官方语言国家的倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究分析LLM的文化能力，但缺乏专门针对LLM在文化相关问题上的区域偏好的研究。

Method: 构建基于综合分类体系的文化相关开放问题（CROQ）数据集，对LLM进行测试分析，并探究文化偏见在训练阶段（预训练 vs 监督微调）的出现时机。

Result: 1) LLM明显倾向于回答与日本相关的问题；2) 使用英语等高资源语言提示时，LLM输出更多样化，且对官方语言为提示语言的国家倾向减弱；3) 文化偏见在监督微调后首次明显出现，而非预训练阶段。

Conclusion: LLM在文化问题上存在特定的区域偏好（尤其是日本），且该偏见主要源于监督微调阶段，为理解LLM文化偏见的来源和机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: LLMs have been showing limitations when it comes to cultural coverage and competence, and in some cases show regional biases such as amplifying Western and Anglocentric viewpoints. While there have been works analysing the cultural capabilities of LLMs, there has not been specific work on highlighting LLM regional preferences when it comes to cultural-related questions. In this work, we propose a new dataset based on a comprehensive taxonomy of Culture-Related Open Questions (CROQ). The results show that, contrary to previous cultural bias work, LLMs show a clear tendency towards countries such as Japan. Moveover, our results show that when prompting in languages such as English or other high-resource ones, LLMs tend to provide more diverse outputs and show less inclinations towards answering questions highlighting countries for which the input language is an official language. Finally, we also investigate at which point of LLM training this cultural bias emerges, with our results suggesting that the first clear signs appear after supervised fine-tuning, and not during pre-training.

</details>


### [45] [AUDITA: A New Dataset to Audit Humans vs. AI Skill at Audio QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21766)
*Tasnim Kabir,Dmytro Kurdydyk,Aadi Palnitkar,Liam Dorn,Ahmed Haj Ahmed,Jordan Lee Boyd-Graber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AUDITA是一个大规模、真实世界的音频问答基准数据集，由人类编写的冷知识问题构成，旨在评估超越表层声学识别的深层音频推理能力。人类平均准确率32.13%，而当前最先进的音频问答模型平均准确率低于8.86%，表明该任务极具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频问答基准存在诸多缺陷：模型可通过捷径策略、短时线索、词汇先验、数据集特定偏差，甚至绕过音频仅凭元数据和文本来成功回答。因此需要构建一个真正需要音频推理能力的基准测试。

Method: 从互联网冷知识中策划由人类撰写的音频相关问题，设计具有挑战性的干扰项和长程时间依赖关系，确保问题无法仅通过孤立文本或声音线索回答。采用项目反应理论(IRT)评估潜在能力、问题难度和模型系统性缺陷。

Result: 人类平均准确率32.13%，最先进音频问答模型平均准确率低于8.86%。IRT分析揭示了模型和数据存在的系统性缺陷。

Conclusion: 现有模型在真实音频推理任务上表现极差，与人类存在巨大差距；AUDITA可作为评估和推动音频推理能力的有效基准。

Abstract: Existing audio question answering benchmarks largely emphasize sound event classification or caption-grounded queries, often enabling models to succeed through shortcut strategies, short-duration cues, lexical priors, dataset-specific biases, or even bypassing audio via metadata and captions rather than genuine reasoning Thus, we present AUDITA (Audio Understanding from Diverse Internet Trivia Authors), a large-scale, real-world benchmark to rigorously evaluate audio reasoning beyond surface-level acoustic recognition. AUDITA comprises carefully curated, human-authored trivia questions grounded in real-world audio, designed to stress robust auditory reasoning through challenging distractors and long-range temporal dependencies, using probing queries that cannot be answered from isolated text or sound cues alone. Human average accuracy of 32.13% shows both the challenge of the task while demonstrating meaningful comprehension of the audio. In stark contrast, state of-the-art audio question answering models perform poorly, with average accuracy below 8.86%. Beyond raw accuracy, we apply Item Response Theory (IRT) to estimate latent proficiency, question difficulty, and expose systematic deficiencies of the models and data.

</details>


### [46] [SemEval-2026 Task 4: Narrative Story Similarity and Narrative Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21782)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Ekaterina Artemova,Haimo Paul Stiemer,Evelyn Gius,Chris Biemann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NSNRL共享任务，通过二分类问题定义叙事相似性（判断两个故事中哪个与锚点故事更相似），构建了超1000个故事摘要三元组的标注数据集，并评估了71个参赛系统的叙事嵌入表示。LLM集成在分类任务中表现最佳，而预训练嵌入模型的预处理/后处理与微调方案在嵌入任务中表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏一个与叙事理论和直觉判断兼容的、可操作的叙事相似性定义，以及相应的基准数据集和评估框架来推动叙事表示学习的发展。

Method: 将叙事相似性操作化为二分类问题（三元组比较），基于新定义的叙事相似性概念收集人工标注（超1000个故事摘要三元组，每个标注至少两位标注者一致同意），并设置分类和嵌入两个赛道评估自动化系统。

Result: 收到46个团队的71个最终提交。分类赛道中LLM集成系统表现最佳；嵌入赛道中预训练嵌入模型的预处理/后处理方案与定制微调方案性能相当。分析表明两个赛道均存在改进空间。

Conclusion: NSNRL任务为叙事相似性研究提供了标准化的评估基准，数据集和可视化资源已公开，并揭示了当前自动化系统在叙事理解上的局限性。

Abstract: We present the shared task on narrative similarity and narrative representation learning - NSNRL (pronounced "nass-na-rel"). The task operationalizes narrative similarity as a binary classification problem: determining which of two stories is more similar to an anchor story. We introduce a novel definition of narrative similarity, compatible with both narrative theory and intuitive judgment. Based on the similarity judgments collected under this concept, we also evaluate narrative embedding representations. We collected at least two annotations each for more than 1,000 story summary triples, with each annotation being backed by at least two annotators in agreement. This paper describes the sampling and annotation process for the dataset; further, we give an overview of the submitted systems and the techniques they employ. We received a total of 71 final submissions from 46 teams across our two tracks. In our triple-based classification setup, LLM ensembles make up many of the top-scoring systems, while in the embedding setup, systems with pre- and post-processing on pretrained embedding models perform about on par with custom fine-tuned solutions. Our analysis identifies potential headroom for improvement of automated systems in both tracks. The task website includes visualizations of embeddings alongside instance-level classification results for all teams.

</details>


### [47] [Machine Behavior in Relational Moral Dilemmas: Moral Rightness, Predicted Human Behavior, and Model Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21871)
*Jiseon Kim,Jea Kwon,Luiz Felipe Vecchietti,Wenchao Dong,Jaehong Kim,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过“举报者困境”实验，研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在道德判断中如何权衡犯罪严重性与人际关系亲密度。结果发现，模型在道德正确性判断上保持公平导向，但其对人类行为的预测会随关系亲密增加转向忠诚导向，而模型自身的决策却与道德正确性一致，而非其行为预测，揭示了LLMs决策中刚性规则优先于社会敏感性的内在不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类道德判断具有情境依赖性和人际关系的调节作用，随着LLMs越来越多地作为决策支持系统，确定它们是否编码了这些社会细微差别至关重要。

Method: 使用举报者困境范式，通过改变犯罪严重性和关系亲密度两个实验维度，评估了三种视角：（1）道德正确性（规范性规范）；（2）预测的人类行为（描述性社会期望）；（3）自主模型决策。并分析了模型的推理过程。

Result: 存在清晰的跨视角分歧：道德正确性始终保持公平导向，而预测的人类行为随关系亲密度增加显著转向忠诚导向。模型自身的决策与道德正确性判断一致，而非其自身的行为预测。

Conclusion: LLMs的决策优先考虑僵化的规范性规则，而非其内部世界建模中的社会敏感性，这种不一致可能导致实际部署中的严重错配。

Abstract: Human moral judgment is context-dependent and modulated by interpersonal relationships. As large language models (LLMs) increasingly function as decision-support systems, determining whether they encode these social nuances is critical. We characterize machine behavior using the Whistleblower's Dilemma by varying two experimental dimensions: crime severity and relational closeness. Our study evaluates three distinct perspectives: (1) moral rightness (prescriptive norms), (2) predicted human behavior (descriptive social expectations), and (3) autonomous model decision-making. By analyzing the reasoning processes, we identify a clear cross-perspective divergence: while moral rightness remains consistently fairness-oriented, predicted human behavior shifts significantly toward loyalty as relational closeness increases. Crucially, model decisions align with moral rightness judgments rather than their own behavioral predictions. This inconsistency suggests that LLM decision-making prioritizes rigid, prescriptive rules over the social sensitivity present in their internal world-modeling, which poses a gap that may lead to significant misalignments in real-world deployments.

</details>


### [48] [Revisiting Non-Verbatim Memorization in Large Language Models: The Role of Entity Surface Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21882)
*Yuto Nishida,Naoki Shikoda,Yosuke Kishinami,Ryo Fujii,Makoto Morishita,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RedirectQA数据集，利用维基百科重定向信息关联多表面形式的实体事实三元组，通过13个LLM的实验发现模型的事实记忆对实体表面形式敏感，不同类别表面形式（别名、缩写、拼写变体等）的鲁棒性不同，表明非逐字记忆既非完全表面特定也非完全表面不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于实体的QA评估通常使用单一规范表面形式查询事实记忆，无法区分事实记忆本身与通过特定名称访问的能力，因此需要引入多样化的表面形式来更准确评估LLM的非逐字记忆。

Method: 构建RedirectQA数据集，利用Wikipedia重定向信息将Wikidata事实三元组与分类的表面形式（别名、缩写、拼写变体、常见错误形式等）关联；在13个LLM上进行表面条件化的事实记忆实验，分析不同表面形式类别下的预测一致性和频率关联。

Result: 模型预测结果常随表面形式变化而变化，且这种不一致性与表面形式类别相关：对轻微拼写变体更鲁棒，对别名和缩写等词汇层面变化更敏感。实体级和表面级频率均与准确率相关，且实体频率的贡献通常超过表面频率。

Conclusion: LLM的事实记忆既非纯粹表面特定也非完全表面不变，表面形式的多样性对评估非逐字记忆至关重要。

Abstract: Understanding what kinds of factual knowledge large language models (LLMs) memorize is essential for evaluating their reliability and limitations. Entity-based QA is a common framework for analyzing non-verbatim memorization, but typical evaluations query each entity using a single canonical surface form, making it difficult to disentangle fact memorization from access through a particular name. We introduce RedirectQA, an entity-based QA dataset that uses Wikipedia redirect information to associate Wikidata factual triples with categorized surface forms for each entity, including alternative names, abbreviations, spelling variants, and common erroneous forms. Across 13 LLMs, we examine surface-conditioned factual memorization and find that prediction outcomes often change when only the entity surface form changes. This inconsistency is category-dependent: models are more robust to minor orthographic variations than to larger lexical variations such as aliases and abbreviations. Frequency analyses further suggest that both entity- and surface-level frequencies are associated with accuracy, and that entity frequency often contributes beyond surface frequency. Overall, factual memorization appears neither purely surface-specific nor fully surface-invariant, highlighting the importance of surface-form diversity in evaluating non-verbatim memorization.

</details>


### [49] [A Multimodal Text- and Graph-Based Approach for Open-Domain Event Extraction from Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21885)
*Praval Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MODEE方法，结合图学习与大语言模型文本表示，解决开放域事件提取中缺乏文档级推理及大模型应用不足的问题，在开放域和封闭域任务上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件提取方法存在两个局限：(1)封闭域算法受限于预定义事件类型，难以泛化到未见类型；(2)开放域算法未充分利用大语言模型的先进能力，且未显式建模文档级别的上下文、结构和语义推理，而这些推理因大模型的"中间丢失"现象和注意力稀释问题而具有挑战性。

Method: 提出MODEE（多模态开放域事件提取），将基于图的表示学习与大语言模型的文本表示相结合，建模文档级推理，实现开放域事件提取。

Result: 在大型数据集上的实证评估表明，MODEE在开放域事件提取上优于最先进方法，并能泛化到封闭域事件提取，同样超越现有算法。

Conclusion: MODEE通过融合图学习与LLM文本表示，有效解决了开放域事件提取中的文档级推理问题，在开放域和封闭域事件提取任务上均取得了最优性能。

Abstract: Event extraction is essential for event understanding and analysis. It supports tasks such as document summarization and decision-making in emergency scenarios. However, existing event extraction approaches have limitations: (1) closed-domain algorithms are restricted to predefined event types and thus rarely generalize to unseen types and (2) open-domain event extraction algorithms, capable of handling unconstrained event types, have largely overlooked the potential of large language models (LLMs) despite their advanced abilities. Additionally, they do not explicitly model document-level contextual, structural, and semantic reasoning, which are crucial for effective event extraction but remain challenging for LLMs due to lost-in-the-middle phenomenon and attention dilution. To address these limitations, we propose multimodal open-domain event extraction, MODEE , a novel approach for open-domain event extraction that combines graph-based learning with text-based representation from LLMs to model document-level reasoning. Empirical evaluations on large datasets demonstrate that MODEE outperforms state-of-the-art open-domain event extraction approaches and can be generalized to closed-domain event extraction, where it outperforms existing algorithms.

</details>


### [50] [TingIS: Real-time Risk Event Discovery from Noisy Customer Incidents at Enterprise Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21889)
*Jun Wang,Ziyin Zhang,Rui Wang,Hang Yu,Peng Di,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TingIS是一个面向企业级云原生服务的端到端事故发现系统，通过多阶段事件链接引擎（结合高效索引与LLM）、级联路由机制和多维降噪管道，在峰值吞吐量每分钟2000+消息的生产环境中实现P90告警延迟3.5分钟、高优先级事故发现率95%，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户事故数据虽然包含监控遗漏的关键风险信号，但存在极端噪声、高吞吐量和多业务线语义复杂性等挑战，亟需从海量嘈杂的用户描述中稳定提取可操作的事故情报。

Method: 提出TingIS系统，核心为多阶段事件链接引擎，协同高效索引技术与LLM进行事件合并决策；辅以级联路由机制实现精准业务归属，以及集成领域知识、统计模式和行为过滤的多维降噪管道。

Result: 在生产环境（峰值>2000条/分钟，日均30万条消息）中，P90告警延迟3.5分钟，高优先级事故发现率95%；在真实数据构建的基准测试中，路由准确率、聚类质量和信噪比均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: TingIS作为企业级端到端事故发现系统，有效解决了大规模云原生服务中实时事故检测的噪声与语义复杂性挑战，实现了高发现率与低延迟的平衡。

Abstract: Real-time detection and mitigation of technical anomalies are critical for large-scale cloud-native services, where even minutes of downtime can result in massive financial losses and diminished user trust. While customer incidents serve as a vital signal for discovering risks missed by monitoring, extracting actionable intelligence from this data remains challenging due to extreme noise, high throughput, and semantic complexity of diverse business lines. In this paper, we present TingIS, an end-to-end system designed for enterprise-grade incident discovery. At the core of TingIS is a multi-stage event linking engine that synergizes efficient indexing techniques with Large Language Models (LLMs) to make informed decisions on event merging, enabling the stable extraction of actionable incidents from just a handful of diverse user descriptions. This engine is complemented by a cascaded routing mechanism for precise business attribution and a multi-dimensional noise reduction pipeline that integrates domain knowledge, statistical patterns, and behavioral filtering. Deployed in a production environment handling a peak throughput of over 2,000 messages per minute and 300,000 messages per day, TingIS achieves a P90 alert latency of 3.5 minutes and a 95\% discovery rate for high-priority incidents. Benchmarks constructed from real-world data demonstrate that TingIS significantly outperforms baseline methods in routing accuracy, clustering quality, and Signal-to-Noise Ratio.

</details>


### [51] [EVENT5Ws: A Large Dataset for Open-Domain Event Extraction from Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21890)
*Praval Sharma,Ashok Samal,Leen-Kiat Soh,Deepti Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了EVENT5Ws，一个大规模、人工标注且经统计验证的开放域事件抽取数据集，并基于该数据集评估了现有大语言模型，建立了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件抽取数据集存在两大局限：封闭域下事件类型覆盖有限，开放域下缺乏大规模人工验证的数据集。为弥补这一空白，需要构建一个高质量、大规模的开放域事件抽取数据集。

Method: 设计了系统化的标注流程来创建EVENT5Ws数据集，并提供了标注复杂性的实证分析。使用该数据集评估了多个预训练大语言模型，并建立了基准。

Result: 在EVENT5Ws上评估了最先进的预训练大语言模型并建立了基准；实验表明在该数据集上训练的模型能够有效泛化到不同地理背景的数据集。

Conclusion: 总结了数据集开发过程中的经验教训，并为未来大规模数据集开发提供了建议。EVENT5Ws有助于开发具有泛化能力的事件抽取算法。

Abstract: Event extraction identifies the central aspects of events from text. It supports event understanding and analysis, which is crucial for tasks such as informed decision-making in emergencies. Therefore, it is necessary to develop automated event extraction approaches. However, existing datasets for algorithm development have limitations, including limited coverage of event types in closed-domain settings and a lack of large, manually verified dataset in open-domain settings. To address these limitations, we create EVENT5Ws , a large, manually annotated, and statistically verified open-domain event extraction dataset. We design a systematic annotation pipeline to create the dataset and provide empirical insights into annotation complexity. Using EVENT5Ws, we evaluate state-of-the-art pre-trained large language models and establish a benchmark for future research. We further show that models trained on EVENT5Ws generalize effectively to datasets from different geographical contexts, which demonstrates its potential for developing generalizable algorithms. Finally, we summarize the lessons learned during the dataset development and provide recommendations to support future large-scale dataset development.

</details>


### [52] [Mapping the Political Discourse in the Brazilian Chamber of Deputies: A Multi-Faceted Computational Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21897)
*Flávio Soriano,Victoria F. Mello,Pedro B. Rigueira,Gisele L. Pappa,Wagner Meira,Ana Paula Couto da Silva,Jussara M. Almeida*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合历时风格分析、上下文主题建模和语义聚类的计算框架，用于分析议会话语的三个维度（如何说、说什么、谁在说），并对巴西众议院2003-2025年超45万篇演讲进行大规模案例研究，发现长期风格趋于简短直接、议程在危机中急剧转向、话语联盟中地域和性别身份常比党派更具显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统立法行为分析过度依赖投票记录，忽视了政治演讲中丰富的语义和修辞内容，本研究旨在通过多维话语分析弥补这一空白。

Method: 提出一个可扩展且可推广的计算框架，整合了历时风格分析（stylometric analysis）、上下文主题建模（contextual topic modeling）以及议员演讲的语义聚类（semantic clustering），用于回答三个互补问题：如何说、说什么、谁在说。

Result: 发现长期风格转变（演讲趋于简短直接）；立法议程在国家危机时出现急剧转向；话语联盟图显示地域和性别身份往往比正式党派归属更具区分度。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种稳健的多维议会话语分析方法，能够有效补充传统基于投票的立法行为研究。

Abstract: Analyses of legislative behavior often rely on voting records, overlooking the rich semantic and rhetorical content of political speech. In this paper, we ask three complementary questions about parliamentary discourse: how things are said, what is being said, and who is speaking in discursively similar ways. To answer these questions, we introduce a scalable and generalizable computational framework that combines diachronic stylometric analysis, contextual topic modeling, and semantic clustering of deputies' speeches. We apply this framework to a large-scale case study of the Brazilian Chamber of Deputies, using a corpus of over 450,000 speeches from 2003 to 2025. Our results show a long-term stylistic shift toward shorter and more direct speeches, a legislative agenda that reorients sharply in response to national crises, and a granular map of discursive alignments in which regional and gender identities often prove more salient than formal party affiliation. More broadly, this work offers a robust methodology for analyzing parliamentary discourse as a multidimensional phenomenon that complements traditional vote-based approaches.

</details>


### [53] [GiVA: Gradient-Informed Bases for Vector-Based Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21901)
*Neeraj Gangwar,Rishabh Deshmukh,Michael Shavlovsky,Hancao Li,Vivek Mittal,Lexing Ying,Nickvash Kani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GiVA是一种基于梯度的初始化策略，专为向量适配方法设计，在保持极端参数效率的同时，将训练时间降低至与LoRA相当的水平，并将所需秩降低了8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量适配方法虽具有极端参数效率，但需要比LoRA更高的秩才能匹配其性能，导致训练成本增加。作者旨在解决这一效率与性能之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出GiVA（Gradient-based Initialization for Vector-based Adaptation），一种基于梯度的初始化策略，用于向量适配方法，使训练时间与LoRA相当，同时保持向量适配的极端参数效率。

Result: 在自然语言理解、自然语言生成和图像分类等多个基准测试中，GiVA一致优于或与现有向量适配方法及LoRA性能相当，同时将秩需求降低了8倍。

Conclusion: GiVA有效弥合了向量适配方法与LoRA之间的性能差距，以更低的秩需求实现了与LoRA相当的训练效率和竞争性能，为参数高效微调提供了更优选择。

Abstract: As model sizes continue to grow, parameter-efficient fine-tuning has emerged as a powerful alternative to full fine-tuning. While LoRA is widely adopted among these methods, recent research has explored vector-based adaptation methods due to their extreme parameter efficiency. However, these methods typically require substantially higher ranks than LoRA to match its performance, leading to increased training costs. This work introduces GiVA, a gradient-based initialization strategy for vector-based adaptation. It achieves training times comparable to LoRA and maintains the extreme parameter efficiency of vector-based adaptation. We evaluate GiVA across diverse benchmarks, including natural language understanding, natural language generation, and image classification. Experiments show that our approach consistently outperforms or achieves performance competitive with existing vector-based adaptation methods and LoRA while reducing rank requirements by a factor of eight ($8\times$).

</details>


### [54] [MathDuels: Evaluating LLMs as Problem Posers and Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21916)
*Zhiqiu Xu,Shibo Jin,Shreya Arya,Mayur Naik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MathDuels基准，模型同时扮演出题者和解题者双重角色，通过自对弈评估揭示传统静态基准无法区分的模型能力差异，且基准难度随参与者能力共同进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学基准因模型性能趋近天花板而无法有效区分模型能力，因其仅将模型视为固定问题集的求解者。

Method: 采用自我对弈框架，模型双重角色（出题/解题）；三阶段生成流水线（元提示、问题生成、难度放大）；独立验证器排除不合理问题；Rasch模型联合估计解题能力和问题难度。

Result: 19个前沿模型实验表明出题与解题能力部分解耦，双角色评估揭示了单角色基准无法区分的差异；新模型能生成击败先前主导模型的问题，基准难度随参与者能力共同进化。

Conclusion: 双角色评估范式能持续区分模型能力，避免基准饱和，为模型评估提供动态进化方案。

Abstract: As frontier language models attain near-ceiling performance on static mathematical benchmarks, existing evaluations are increasingly unable to differentiate model capabilities, largely because they cast models solely as solvers of fixed problem sets. We introduce MathDuels, a self-play benchmark in which models occupy dual roles: each authors math problems under adversarial prompting and solves problems authored by every other participant. Problems are produced through a three-stage generation pipeline (meta-prompting, problem generation, and difficulty amplification), and validated by an independent verifier that excludes ill-posed questions. A Rasch model (Rasch, 1993) jointly estimates solver abilities and problem difficulties; author quality is derived from the difficulties of each model's authored problems. Experiments across 19 frontier models reveal that authoring and solving capabilities are partially decoupled, and that dual-role evaluation reveals capability separations invisible in single-role benchmarks. As newer models enter the arena, they produce problems that defeat previously dominant solvers, so the benchmark's difficulty co-evolves with participant strength rather than saturating at a fixed ceiling. We host a public leaderboard that updates as new models are released.

</details>


### [55] [Evaluation of Automatic Speech Recognition Using Generative Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21928)
*Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Shashi Kumar,Driss Khalil,Sergio Burdisso,Petr Motlicek,Shiran Liu,Mickael Rouvier,Jane Wottawa,Richard Dufour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探索了解码器型大语言模型（LLMs）在语义级ASR评估中的三种应用方法，在HATS数据集上最佳LLM与人工标注的一致性达92-94%，远超WER的63%及现有语义指标，表明LLM在可解释的语义ASR评估中具有广阔前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR评估指标WER对语义不敏感，嵌入型语义指标虽有改进但解码器型LLM在该任务中的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 通过三种方法评估LLM在ASR评估中的相关性：(1) 从两个候选假设中选择最佳假设；(2) 使用生成式嵌入计算语义距离；(3) 对错误进行定性分类。

Result: 在HATS数据集上，最佳LLM在假设选择任务中与人工标注的一致性达92-94%，WER仅为63%；解码器型LLM的嵌入性能与编码器模型相当。

Conclusion: LLM为可解释且具有语义感知的ASR评估提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) is traditionally evaluated using Word Error Rate (WER), a metric that is insensitive to meaning. Embedding-based semantic metrics are better correlated with human perception, but decoder-based Large Language Models (LLMs) remain underexplored for this task. This paper evaluates their relevance through three approaches: (1) selecting the best hypothesis between two candidates, (2) computing semantic distance using generative embeddings, and (3) qualitative classification of errors. On the HATS dataset, the best LLMs achieve 92--94\% agreement with human annotators for hypothesis selection, compared to 63\% for WER, also outperforming semantic metrics. Embeddings from decoder-based LLMs show performance comparable to encoder models. Finally, LLMs offer a promising direction for interpretable and semantic ASR evaluation.

</details>

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本研讨会提供了关于非线性规划、变分不等式和互补系统的紧凑而严谨的介绍，重点解释了MPEC一阶最优性条件成立的数学逻辑，以及如何在研究实践中应用该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在填补理论与实践之间的鸿沟，帮助研究人员理解MPEC一阶最优性条件成立的数学逻辑，并掌握如何在实际研究中选择验证路径、证明假设、推导正确的一阶分析。

Method: 通过紧凑而严谨的方式介绍非线性规划、变分不等式和互补系统的基本语言，从数学逻辑出发解释假设条件，并提供实践指导路径。

Result: 提供了MPEC一阶最优性条件有效性的理论基础，以及一套用于研究实践的分类、验证和推导方法。

Conclusion: 该研讨会有助于研究人员系统理解MPEC理论并在实践中正确运用一阶分析工具。

Abstract: In this workshop, we present a compact but rigorous introduction to the basic language of nonlinear programming, variational inequalities, and complementarity systems. The goal is twofold. First, we explain the mathematical logic of hypotheses under which first-order optimality conditions for MPECs become valid. Second, we explain how to use that theory in research practice: how to classify a model, choose the appropriate verification route, prove the right hypotheses, and derive a correct first-order analysis.

</details>


### [57] [Optimization Workshop Notes for Mathematical Programming with Equilibrium Constraints (MPECs): Second-Order Optimality Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20992)
*Jiguang Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提供了一个关于数学规划含平衡约束（MPEC）二阶最优性条件的紧凑而严谨的导引，从经典非线性规划出发，解释了为什么在平衡约束条件下经典理论失效，并发展了三种主要观点：基于乘子的条件、基于下层平衡系统解映射的隐式规划条件、以及通过将互补结构分解为光滑片段得到的分段规划条件。重点放在概念结构、临界锥、强正则性以及曲率项的确切作用上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPEC问题的二阶最优性条件在经典非线性规划框架下会失效，需要发展专门的理论来刻画最优性条件，特别是临界锥、强正则性和曲率项的作用。

Method: 从经典非线性规划模板出发，提出三种MPEC二阶最优性条件的分析视角：(i)基于乘子的条件,(ii)基于下层平衡系统解映射的隐式规划条件,(iii)通过分解互补结构为光滑片段得到的分段规划条件。

Result: 系统性地阐明了MPEC二阶最优性条件的三种主流分析框架及其内在联系。

Conclusion: 为理解和应用MPEC的二阶最优性条件提供了概念性框架，突出了临界锥、强正则性和曲率项在其中的核心作用。

Abstract: In this workshop, we present a compact but rigorous introduction to second-order optimality conditions for mathematical programs with equilibrium constraints (MPECs). We start from the classical nonlinear programming template, then explain why it fails in the equilibrium-constrained setting, and develop the three main viewpoints used in the literature: (i) multiplier-based conditions, (ii) implicit-programming conditions based on the solution map of the lower-level equilibrium system, and (iii) piecewise-programming conditions obtained by decomposing complementarity structure into smooth pieces. The emphasis is on conceptual structure, critical cones, strong regularity, and the exact role of curvature terms.

</details>


### [58] [Integrated packing, placement, scheduling, and routing of personalized production: a pharmaceutical Industry 4.0 use-case with a planar transport system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21029)
*Viktor Emil Korladinov,Antonin Novak,Zdeněk Hanzálek,Erik Sonntag,František Štěpánek*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于平面网格的柔性制造系统（FMS）框架，用于解决个性化药物生产中的内部物流与生产作业协同调度问题。该框架在战术层面通过混合整数二次规划（MIQP）解决布局与设备放置问题，在操作层面通过约束规划（CP）和DAG冲突消解机制实现移动器的无冲突路径调度。基于40种药物的真实处方数据实验表明，该框架在多种布局拓扑下可高效处理最多500个订单，且计算时间满足日常运营需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统串行生产线在内部物流与生产作业协同调度中存在效率低下的问题，而新兴的平面运输系统（如基于瓷砖网格的独立移动器）具有全二维自由度，但缺乏相应的调度框架。同时，制药行业个性化药物的自动化生产对该类系统有实际需求。

Method: 战术层面：采用混合整数二次规划（MIQP）模型求解包装问题，利用历史患者数据中的药物共现模式；针对放置问题，构建结合分配问题与最短哈密顿路径（含邻域）的双层优化模型来最小化预期运输距离。操作层面：使用约束规划（CP）将移动器建模为水库资源以保证订单完整性，再通过基于DAG的迭代冲突消解机制将调度方案转化为无冲突路径。

Result: 使用40种药物的真实处方数据进行评估，在多种布局拓扑下，该框架能够高效处理最多500个订单，生成的调度方案高效且计算时间在每日运营可接受范围内。

Conclusion: 平面FMS框架能有效解决个性化药物生产中的协同调度问题，在战术和操作两个层面均具有计算可行性和调度效果，为制药行业自动化生产提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The recent emergence of planar transport systems necessitates re-evaluation of Flexible Manufacturing Systems (FMS) to address the simultaneous scheduling of internal logistics and production operations. By operating on a tile-based planar grid, these systems allow independent movers full two-dimensional freedom, mitigating inefficiencies inherent to traditional sequential lines. This paper applies a planar FMS framework to a real-world use case in the pharmaceutical industry: the automated production of personalized drugs.
  Implementing this system requires solving optimization problems at both tactical and operational levels. The tactical level involves decisions regarding production line layout and the positioning of drug dispensers. A Mixed-Integer Quadratic Programming model is utilized for the packing problem to exploit drug co-occurrence patterns found in historical patient data. Subsequently, we solve the placement problem - a bi-level problem combining an assignment problem with Shortest Hamiltonian paths with neighborhoods - to arrange dispensers in a layout minimizing expected travel distances.
  The operational level is encountered daily, scheduling individual movers to process new orders as quickly as possible. This scheduling problem is formulated using Constraint Programming, modeling movers as reservoir resources to ensure order completeness, complemented by a routing phase using an iterative conflict-resolution mechanism and DAG-based reasoning to convert schedules into conflict-free paths.
  Evaluation using real-world prescription data for 40 drugs shows the framework scales efficiently across several layout topologies for up to 500 orders, with schedules that are highly effective and computationally tractable for daily operations.

</details>


### [59] [Modeling Gaps in Hydropower Cascading System Models: A Systematic Review of Rule-Based Formulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21062)
*Quentin Ploussard,Lukas Livengood,Slaven Kincic*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文综述了131篇关于梯级水电站系统优化调度的文献，建立了一个包含10个方程的通用数学框架作为标准模型，系统评估了每篇文献对物理关系的保留、简化或省略情况，论证了混合整数线性规划结合分段线性逼近是物理精度与计算可行性的最佳平衡，并通过文献计量分析指出高性能开源求解器的缺失是制约可复现性的关键障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着间歇性可再生能源占比提升，高保真水电站建模已成为保障电力系统可靠性、长期能源韧性和经济性的核心需求。梯级水电站的协调调度涉及多水库物理过程、严格环境约束和动态市场参与之间的复杂平衡，亟需系统性审视现有优化方法的建模保真度。

Method: 对131篇代表性文献进行系统性综述，建立包含10个方程的通用数学模型作为基准，涵盖时空入流路由、库容-水位关系、水头依赖发电和禁止运行区等物理特性。逐篇对照标准模型进行普查，记录每篇文献对各项物理关系的处理方式（保留、简化或省略），并结合电网可靠性视角评估建模简化带来的影响。同时进行求解器使用情况的文献计量分析。

Result: 明确了混合整数线性规划结合分段线性逼近方法在物理精度与计算可行性之间的最优平衡，指出最优回归技术可最小化组合复杂性开销。文献计量分析揭示高性能开源求解器几乎缺失，成为制约模型可复现性和广泛采用的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 梯级水电站优化调度研究需在建模保真度上进一步提升，混合整数线性规划结合分段线性逼近是当前最佳实践范式；开源高性能求解器的缺乏是亟待解决的可复现性障碍，也是推动高保真模型广泛应用的潜在突破口。

Abstract: The coordination of cascading hydropower systems represents a fundamental challenge in modern energy systems engineering, requiring a sophisticated balance between multi-reservoir physics, stringent environmental regulations, and dynamic market participation. As intermittent energy sources increase, the transition to high-fidelity hydropower modeling has become a core requirement for ensuring power system reliability, long-term energy resilience and affordability. This review provides a comprehensive analysis of 131 seminal articles through the exclusive lens of optimization-based approaches, intentionally omitting pure simulation and heuristic methods to focus on rigorous mathematical formulations. A generalized 10-equation mathematical framework is established as a formal baseline, capturing the full physical and hydraulic behavior of cascading systems, including spatiotemporal inflow routing, storage-to-elevation relationships, head-dependent power generation, and prohibited operating zones. Each article is evaluated against every equation of this Standard Model in a systematic census documenting which physical relationships are included, simplified, or omitted, providing an empirical measure of modeling fidelity across the field. Modeling simplifications are evaluated through the lens of grid reliability rather than water management performance alone. The review makes a focused technical case for mixed-integer linear programming with piecewise linear approximations as the optimal balance between physical accuracy and computational tractability, highlighting recent optimal regression techniques that minimize combinatorial overhead. Finally, a bibliometric analysis of solver usage identifies the near-absence of high-performance open-source solvers as a critical reproducibility barrier, and a promising avenue for broader adoption of high-fidelity cascading hydropower models.

</details>


### [60] [The Method of Ellipcenters for Strongly Convex Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21132)
*Yunier Bello-Cruz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将椭圆中心法（ME）从强凸二次极小化扩展到更广泛的强凸且Lipschitz连续梯度函数类，证明其收敛率与带精确线搜索的梯度下降法相当，并在特定条件下实现每步改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将椭圆中心法（ME）从强凸二次极小化问题推广到更一般的强凸函数类（具有Lipschitz连续梯度），并理论分析其收敛性能。

Method: 每步迭代使用两次梯度计算（当前点+水平集伴随点），利用水平集对称性的中点论证，在两梯度方向线性无关时进一步改进收敛步长。

Result: ME收敛率与带精确线搜索的梯度下降法匹配；当两梯度夹角一致远离零时，可实现全局加速；在二维空间中至多两步收敛。

Conclusion: ME在更广函数类上保持高效收敛，理论结果与正则化逻辑回归数值实验一致。

Abstract: The Method of Ellipcenters (ME), introduced in~\cite{ME2025} for strongly convex quadratic minimization, uses two gradient evaluations per iteration: one at the current iterate and one at a companion point on the same level set. We extend ME to the broader class of strongly convex functions with Lipschitz continuous gradient, and prove that ME matches the convergence rate of gradient descent with exact line search on this class. When the two gradient directions are linearly independent, a midpoint argument exploiting the level-set symmetry yields a further per-step improvement, which is global when the angle between the two gradients is uniformly bounded away from zero. ME also converges in at most two steps in dimension two. Numerical experiments on regularized logistic regression confirm the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [61] [Revisiting Subgradient Dominance in Robust MDPs: Counterexamples, Hardness, and Sufficient Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21177)
*Toshinori Kitamura,Arnob Ghosh,Alex Ayoub,Thang D. Chu,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明投影次梯度下降（PSD）用于求解鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）时，先前声称的次梯度优势性质不成立，即使在小规模不确定性集下也存在次优严格局部极小值，且找到ε-最优策略是NP难的；同时识别了两种使RMDP满足次梯度优势的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作声称PSD方法在一般紧致不确定性集下具有次梯度优势性质，能以多项式收敛到ε-最优策略。本文旨在检验这一论断的正确性。

Method: 通过反例构造（基数2的不确定性集）证明次梯度优势不成立；通过归约证明NP难性；识别两种保证次梯度优势的条件（唯一最坏情况转移核或唯一最坏情况动作值函数）。

Result: (1) RMDP目标函数不满足次梯度优势，存在次优严格局部极小值；(2) 在有限转移不确定性集和有限成本不确定性集下，寻找ε-最优策略是NP难的；(3) 当每个策略的最坏情况转移核或最坏情况动作值函数唯一时，RMDP满足次梯度优势。

Conclusion: PSD方法在RMDP上的收敛性分析需要更严格的假设，现有结论存在根本性错误；两种唯一性条件可作为后续算法设计的理论基础。

Abstract: Projected subgradient descent (PSD) has gained popularity for solving robust Markov decision processes (RMDPs) because it applies to a broader class of uncertainty sets than traditional dynamic programming. Existing work claims that RMDPs with a general compact uncertainty set satisfy the subgradient dominance property, under which exact PSD converges to an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy in a polynomial number of updates (e.g., Wang et al., 2023). We show that these claims are incorrect. Even when the uncertainty set has cardinality two, the RMDP objective is not subgradient-dominant and can admit suboptimal strict local minima. Moreover, we prove that finding an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy can be NP-hard even in settings where subgradients are efficiently computable: (i) finite transition uncertainty sets and (ii) $sa$-rectangular finite transition uncertainty sets with finite cost uncertainty sets. Finally, we identify two conditions under which RMDPs do satisfy subgradient dominance: when, for each policy, either the worst-case transition kernel or the worst-case action-value function is unique.

</details>

Method: 通过推导正则化离散时间贝尔曼方程诱导的最优策略与连续时间随机控制问题真实最优反馈控制之间的定量收敛速率，建立两者之间的严格联系。

Conclusion: 该工作为在连续时间随机环境中理解和实现探索性RL策略的稳定性提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: This paper establishes a rigorous connection between regularized discrete-time reinforcement learning (RL) and continuous-time stochastic optimal control. Specifically, classical RL algorithms are typically solving a regularized discrete-time Bellman equation. We study the discretization error, namely, the gap between the optimal policy induced by the regularized discrete-time Bellman equation and the true optimal feedback control of the underlying continuous-time stochastic control problem. By deriving quantitative convergence rates for this gap, we provide a rigorous foundation for understanding the stability and implementation of exploratory RL policies in stochastic continuous-time environments.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弦稳定性研究针对特定车辆模型和共识协议碎片化，未能识别决定扰动放大或衰减的根本因素。

Result: 弦稳定性仅由通信丰富度r决定，协议阶数m仅影响中频暂态行为；低频增益与r成反比；弦稳定当且仅当r≥2。

Conclusion: 建立了结构-动态分离原理，统一了经典编队结果，揭示了通信拓扑与控制器设计之间的相互作用，为未来鲁棒扩展提供了基线。

Abstract: This paper presents a unified string-stability framework for leader-follower multi-agent systems governed by first-, second-, and m-th order consensus protocols operating under an r-predecessor directed communication topology. While string stability has been extensively studied for specific vehicle models and individual consensus protocols, existing results remain fragmented across protocol orders and do not identify the fundamental factors governing disturbance amplification or attenuation. This work shows that, for all consensus orders, string stability is dictated solely by the communication richness r, while the protocol order m influences only the mid-frequency transient behavior. In particular, the low-frequency gain of the disturbance propagation coefficient is inversely proportional to r for every m, implying that higher-order consensus cannot overcome the structural limitation imposed by insufficient communication and that, under the adopted H-infinity-based string-stability definition and the present framework, string stability is achievable if and only if r >= 2. This establishes a structural-dynamic separation principle that unifies and generalizes classical platoon results, providing new insight into the interplay between topology and controller design in cooperative driving and multi-agent coordination. The framework is developed under idealized identical-agent and fixed-topology assumptions, providing a baseline for future robust extensions. Numerical simulations corroborate the analysis and illustrate how m and r jointly shape disturbance propagation along the formation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究约束随机非光滑极小极大优化问题，提出了无需弱凸性假设的投影无梯度下降上升方法，并建立了求解Goldstein鞍点和广义Goldstein驻点的非渐近收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非光滑极小极大优化方法通常依赖弱凸性等额外假设，限制了其适用性。本文旨在去除这些假设，为更一般的约束非光滑极小极大问题提供可收敛的求解方法。

Method: 引入(η_x,η_y,δ,ε)-Goldstein鞍点驻点(GSSP)概念刻画收敛性；提出投影无梯度下降上升方法求解GSSP；进一步提出嵌套循环投影无梯度下降上升方法求解(η,δ,ε)-广义Goldstein驻点(GGSP)。

Result: 所提方法在无需弱凸性假设的条件下，建立了求解GSSP和GGSP的非渐近收敛率。

Conclusion: 本文方法有效去除了现有工作对弱凸性的依赖，拓展了非光滑极小极大优化的理论框架。

Abstract: This paper considers constrained stochastic nonsmooth minimax optimization problem of the form $\min_{\mathbf{x}\in\mathcal{X}}\max_{\mathbf{y}\in\mathcal{Y}}f\left(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\right)=\mathbb{E}[F(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y};\mathbfξ)]$, where the objective $f(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})$ is concave in $\mathbf{y}$ but possibly nonconvex in $\mathbf{x}$, the stochastic component $F(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y};\mathbfξ)$ indexed by random variable $\mathbfξ$ is mean-squared Lipschitz continuous, and the feasible sets $\mathcal X$ and $\mathcal Y$ are convex and compact. We introduce the notion of $(η_x,η_y,δ,ε)$-Goldstein saddle stationary point (GSSP) to characterize the convergence for solving constrained nonsmooth minimax problems. We then develop projected gradient-free descent ascent methods for finding $(η_x,η_y,δ,ε)$-GSSPs of the objective function $f(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})$ with non-asymptotic convergence rates. We further propose nested-loop projected gradient-free descent ascent methods to establish the non-asymptotic convergence for finding $(η,δ,ε)$-generalized Goldstein stationary points (GGSP) [Liu et al., 2024] of the primal function $Φ(\mathbf{x})\triangleq\max_{\mathbf{y}\in\mathcal{Y}}{f}\left(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\right)$. It is worth noting that our algorithm designs and theoretical analyses do not require additional assumptions such as the weak convexity used in prior works on nonsmooth minimax optimization [Lin et al., 2025, Boţ and Böhm, 2023].

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出迷你批次随机Halpern算法，通过减小映射计算成本来解决大规模不动点问题，并证明了该算法在均方意义下收敛到初始点在不动点集上的最近点，同时分析了收敛速率与步长设置的关系。

Abstract: The Halpern algorithm is a powerful fixed point approximation method for finding the closest point in the fixed point set of a nonexpansive mapping to the initial point. However, in practice, it is not necessarily true that this algorithm can be applied to large-scale fixed point problems, since the computation of the nonexpansive mapping is expensive. In this paper, we present mini-batch stochastic Halpern algorithm to resolve the issue caused by the computational difficulty of the mapping. We preform a convergence analysis demonstrating that the algorithm with diminishing step sizes and increasing batch sizes converges in mean square to the closest point in the fixed point set to the initial point. We also perform a convergence rate analysis demonstrating that convergence speed of the algorithm depends on the settings of the diminishing step sizes.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a class of super-schemes for efficiently solving nonlinear unconstrained optimization problems. The proposed approach introduces two novel choices of step-size parameters, leading to efficient descent directions without requiring second-order information. We develop one-step, two-step, and three-step iterative schemes (denoted by SS1, SS2, and SS3) and establish that these methods achieve higher-order convergence of orders two, four, and six, respectively. Despite their high convergence rates, the computational complexity of the proposed methods remains comparable to existing gradient-based methods, with a cost of $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ per iteration. The proposed methods are simple to implement and do not require complicated line-search procedures. Their effectiveness is demonstrated through extensive numerical experiments on a wide range of problems, including large-scale and ill-conditioned cases. The results show that the proposed methods significantly outperform classical methods, such as the Barzilai-Borwein method and other gradient-based approaches, in terms of iteration count and computational efficiency. Finally, the numerical results are consistent with the theoretical analysis, confirming the stability of the proposed schemes for test optimization problems.

Abstract: This paper is dedicated to the analysis of infinite horizon optimal control problems subject to semilinear parabolic equations with constraints on the controls and discounted cost functionals. The discount factors on the cost and the state components are allowed to differ from each other. First-order as well as second-order optimality conditions are derived and the importance of allowing different discount factors for the second-order analysis for the class of nonlinearities under consideration is demonstrated. Finally convergence and rate of convergence for the approximation of the infinite horizon problem by a family of finite horizon problems is proven.

Abstract: We study the vanishing-regularization limit of entropically regularized optimal transport (EOT) for the Euclidean distance cost $c(x,y)=\|x-y\|$ in dimension $d>1$. We develop a comprehensive variational convergence framework that entails two main results. First, we resolve the longstanding entropic selection problem: the EOT minimizer converges to a distinguished optimal transport plan that is characterized explicitly as the solution of a constrained EOT problem on each transport ray. Denoting by $\varepsilon>0$ the regularization parameter, this selection holds for all $o(\varepsilon)$-approximate minimizers, with sharp failure at the $O(\varepsilon)$ scale. Second, we establish an explicit second-order expansion of the entropic transport cost. The second-order term encodes the geometry of the regularization and reveals the optimal asymptotic tradeoff between entropy and transport cost.

</details>

Method: (1) 建立了含趋氧项的Keller-Segel型偏微分方程模型；(2) 利用弱-强解理论证明模型适定性；(3) 定义含肿瘤生长与氧气浓度惩罚的成本泛函，证明最优控制存在性并推导一阶最优性条件；(4) 采用伴随方法计算梯度，结合Adam优化算法迭代求解最优控制。

Result: 理论层面：证明了弱-强解的存在唯一性以及全局最优解的存在性，推导了最优性必要条件。数值层面：通过梯度下降（Adam方法）实现了最优治疗方案的逼近，数值实验验证了所提方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的最优控制框架能够有效联合优化化疗与抗血管生成疗法，为胶质母细胞瘤的多模态治疗策略提供了数学理论基础和可行的数值求解方法。

Abstract: We propose and analyze an optimal control problem associated with a Keller-Segel type parabolic system with chemoattraction, modeling the glioblastoma growth in a bi-dimensional bounded domain, influenced by the presence of oxygen where the controls are two different (chemotherapy and antiangiogenic) therapies. The model considers the random diffusion of tumor cells and oxygen, the movement of cells towards the oxygen gradient (oxytaxis), and reaction terms describing the interaction between cells and oxygen. We establish a mathematical framework to analyze the existence and uniqueness of weak-strong solution of the model and subsequently we analyze an optimal control problem considering a cost functional that minimizes both the tumor growth and the oxygen concentration. We prove the existence of a global optimal solution and derive necessary first-order optimality conditions. Finally, we propose a methodology for approximating the optimal therapies. We use the gradient of the reduced cost functional through the adjoint scheme, and minimize the cost functional implementing the Adam gradient optimization method. Some numerical experiments are provided to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed scheme.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设系统状态完全可观测，但在实际应用中控制器往往只能通过观测过程获取部分信息。因此，本文旨在解决部分观测条件下的 $H_{2}/H_{\infty}$ 控制问题，更贴近工程实际。

Method: 将问题建模为非零和微分博弈；利用卡尔曼滤波方法推导最优滤波方程；建立部分观测框架下的随机有界实引理；通过交叉耦合Riccati方程的可解性刻画Nash均衡的存在性条件；最后用无人机数值示例验证方法有效性。

Result: 推导了部分观测下的最优滤波方程；建立了部分观测框架下的随机有界实引理，给出了 $H_\infty$ 鲁棒约束的充要条件；揭示了Nash均衡存在性与交叉耦合Riccati方程可解性之间的关联；无人机算例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将 $H_{2}/H_{\infty}$ 控制问题从完全观测扩展到部分观测场景，为实际工程中仅能获取部分状态信息的系统（如无人机）提供了理论支撑和有效控制方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates the $H_{2}/H_{\infty}$ control problem for linear stochastic differential systems under partial observation. Unlike existing studies that assume full state accessibility, we consider the scenario where the controller has access only to an observation process. The objective is to design a controller that balances the $H_2$ performance criterion with the $H_\infty$ robustness requirement under worst-case disturbances, formulated as a nonzero-sum differential game. Using the Kalman filtering method, we derive the corresponding optimal filtering equation. Furthermore, a Stochastic Bounded Real Lemma under the partial observation framework is established, providing necessary and sufficient conditions for the $H_\infty$ robustness constraint. We also show the connection between the existence of a Nash equilibrium and the solvability of the cross-coupled Riccati equations, and illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach through a numerical example involving an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV).

</details>

*Ji-il Park,Inwook Shim,Chong Hui Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文聚焦未来战争中自动化行动方案（CoA）规划系统的发展需求，在公开信息范围内介绍了相关军事条令，针对CoA规划各阶段提出了适用的AI技术，并最终提出了一种自动化CoA规划系统的架构设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着作战速度、监视范围和武器射程的扩大，传统人工CoA规划日益困难，亟需开发基于AI的自动化CoA规划系统。但由于军事敏感性，相关技术成熟度难以评估，公开信息有限，因此有必要在公开范围内整合条令并探索可行技术方案。

Method: 在公开信息范围内梳理相关军事条令，分析CoA规划各阶段的特点，针对每个阶段提出适用的AI技术，并在此基础上设计自动化CoA规划系统的整体架构。

Result: 提出了适用于CoA规划各阶段的AI技术方案，并设计了一个面向自动化CoA规划的系统架构。

Conclusion: 本研究为未来智能化军事决策支持中的CoA自动化规划提供了技术路线和架构参考，有助于推动AI在军事行动规划领域的实际应用。

Abstract: The automation system for Course of Action (CoA) planning is an essential element in future warfare. As maneuver speeds increase, surveillance ranges extend, and weapon ranges grow, the operational area expands, making traditional manned-based CoA planning increasingly challenging. Consequently, the development of an AI-based automated CoA planning system is becoming increasingly necessary. Accordingly, several countries and defense organizations are actively developing AI-based CoA planning systems. However, due to security restrictions and limited public disclosure, the technical maturity of such systems remains difficult to assess. Furthermore, as these systems are military-related, their details are not publicly disclosed, making it difficult to accurately assess the current level of development. In response to this, this study aims to introduce relevant doctrines within the scope of publicly available information and present applicable AI technologies for each stage of the CoA planning process. Ultimately, it proposes an architecture for the development of an automated CoA planning system.

</details>


### [72] [Escaping the Agreement Trap: Defensibility Signals for Evaluating Rule-Governed AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20972)
*Michael O'Herlihy,Rosa Català*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出在规则治理环境中，传统基于人工标签一致性的内容审核评估方法存在"一致性陷阱"——将合理但不同于历史标签的决策误判为错误。作者提出基于策略正确性的评估框架，引入防御性指数(DI)和模糊性指数(AI)，以及概率防御信号(PDS)来评估推理稳定性。在19.3万条Reddit审核决策上验证，发现一致性与策略正确性指标间存在33-46.6个百分点的差距，79.8-80.6%的模型假阴性实为策略可行决策。构建的治理门控系统实现78.6%自动化覆盖率与64.9%风险降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内容审核评估依赖于与人工标签的一致性，但在规则治理环境中存在根本性缺陷：多个决策可能与政策逻辑一致，而一致性指标会惩罚合理决策并将歧义误判为错误，即"一致性陷阱"(Agreement Trap)。

Method: 提出策略正确性(policy-grounded correctness)评估框架，定义防御性指数(DI)和模糊性指数(AI)；引入概率防御信号(PDS)，利用审计模型token对数概率估计推理稳定性；将LLM推理链作为治理信号，审计模型不直接分类内容是否违规，而是验证决策是否可从规则层级中逻辑推导。

Result: 在19.3万+条Reddit审核决策上验证，一致性与策略正确性指标间差距为33-46.6个百分点；79.8-80.6%的模型假阴性实为策略可行决策；规则细化可将模糊性指数降低10.8个百分点同时保持DI稳定；PDS方差主要由治理歧义而非解码噪声驱动；治理门控系统实现78.6%自动化覆盖率与64.9%风险降低。

Conclusion: 规则治理环境下的评估应从与历史标签的一致性转向基于显式规则的推理有效性，内容审核系统的评估范式需要根本性转变。

Abstract: Content moderation systems are typically evaluated by measuring agreement with human labels. In rule-governed environments this assumption fails: multiple decisions may be logically consistent with the governing policy, and agreement metrics penalize valid decisions while mischaracterizing ambiguity as error - a failure mode we term the Agreement Trap. We formalize evaluation as policy-grounded correctness and introduce the Defensibility Index (DI) and Ambiguity Index (AI). To estimate reasoning stability without additional audit passes, we introduce the Probabilistic Defensibility Signal (PDS), derived from audit-model token logprobs. We harness LLM reasoning traces as a governance signal rather than a classification output by deploying the audit model not to decide whether content violates policy, but to verify whether a proposed decision is logically derivable from the governing rule hierarchy. We validate the framework on 193,000+ Reddit moderation decisions across multiple communities and evaluation cohorts, finding a 33-46.6 percentage-point gap between agreement-based and policy-grounded metrics, with 79.8-80.6% of the model's false negatives corresponding to policy-grounded decisions rather than true errors. We further show that measured ambiguity is driven by rule specificity: auditing 37,286 identical decisions under three tiers of the same community rules reduces AI by 10.8 pp while DI remains stable. Repeated-sampling analysis attributes PDS variance primarily to governance ambiguity rather than decoding noise. A Governance Gate built on these signals achieves 78.6% automation coverage with 64.9% risk reduction. Together, these results show that evaluation in rule-governed environments should shift from agreement with historical labels to reasoning-grounded validity under explicit rules.

</details>


### [73] [Value-Conflict Diagnostics Reveal Widespread Alignment Faking in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20995)
*Inderjeet Nair,Jie Ruan,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VLAF诊断框架，发现对齐伪装现象比以往报告更为普遍（7B模型即可出现），并利用表征空间中的单一对比方向实现轻量级缓解，效果显著（最高减少94%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐伪装检测工具局限于高毒性、明显有害的场景，导致模型直接拒绝响应，无法真正探测模型在开发者策略与自身价值观冲突时的伪装倾向。需构建能绕过拒绝行为、保留有意义思辨空间的诊断方法。

Method: 提出VLAF框架，基于"开发者策略与模型强烈持有价值观冲突时最易发生对齐伪装"假设，使用道德上无歧义的情景探测多种价值观冲突，绕过拒绝行为。进一步发现监督条件引发的激活偏移位于表征空间单一方向，据此构建对比引导向量实现轻量级推理时缓解。

Result: 对齐伪装比以前报告的更普遍，olmo2-7b-instruct在37%案例中伪装对齐。提出的缓解方法无需标注数据且计算开销极小，在olmo2-7b-instruct、olmo2-13b-instruct和qwen3-8b上分别实现85.8%、94.0%和57.7%的相对对齐伪装降低。

Conclusion: 对齐伪装可在小模型中显著发生；其行为差异可被单一表征方向捕获；利用该特性可实现高效无监督缓解。

Abstract: Alignment faking, where a model behaves aligned with developer policy when monitored but reverts to its own preferences when unobserved, is a concerning yet poorly understood phenomenon, in part because current diagnostic tools remain limited. Prior diagnostics rely on highly toxic and clearly harmful scenarios, causing most models to refuse immediately. As a result, models never deliberate over developer policy, monitoring conditions, or the consequences of non-compliance, making these diagnostics fundamentally unable to detect alignment faking propensity. To support study of this phenomenon, we first introduce VLAF, a diagnostic framework grounded in the hypothesis that alignment faking is most likely when developer policy conflicts with a model's strongly held values. VLAF uses morally unambiguous scenarios to probe this conflict across diverse moral values, bypassing refusal behavior while preserving meaningful deliberative stakes. Using VLAF, we find that alignment faking is substantially more prevalent than previously reported, occurring in models as small as 7B parameters - with olmo2-7b-instruct faking alignment in 37% of cases.Finally, we show that oversight conditions induce activation shifts that lie along a single direction in representation space. This means the behavioral divergence driving alignment faking can be captured by a single contrastive steering vector, which we exploit for lightweight inference-time mitigation. Finally, we exploit this for mitigation that requires no labeled data and minimal computational overhead, achieving relative reductions in alignment faking of 85.8%, 94.0%, and 57.7% on olmo2-7b-instruct, olmo2-13b-instruct, and qwen3-8b respectively.

</details>


### [74] [The Last Harness You'll Ever Build](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21003)
*Haebin Seong,Li Yin,Haoran Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个两层级自动化框架，第一层（Harness Evolution Loop）自动优化单个任务的智能体工作架构（提示、工具、编排逻辑），第二层（Meta-Evolution Loop）跨任务学习最优进化协议，使得新任务无需任何人工架构工程即可快速适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体在复杂、领域特定工作流中部署时，每个新任务都需要专家手动设计提示词、工具、编排逻辑和评估标准（即"架构工程"），这一过程耗时耗力且不可扩展。

Method: 两层级框架：（1）Harness Evolution Loop：Worker Agent执行任务，Evaluator Agent对抗式诊断失败并评分，Evolution Agent基于历史修改架构；（2）Meta-Evolution Loop：跨多个不同任务优化进化协议本身（包括Worker、初始架构、Evaluator、Evolution），学习一个能快速收敛到最优架构的元协议。

Result: 形式化了该框架与元学习的对应关系，给出了两个层级的算法。实现了从手动架构工程到自动化架构工程的转变，并进一步自动化了自动化设计本身。

Conclusion: 该框架能够消除新任务场景下的人工架构工程需求，使得智能体可以零人工干预地适应任意新领域。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed on complex, domain-specific workflows -- navigating enterprise web applications that require dozens of clicks and form fills, orchestrating multi-step research pipelines that span search, extraction, and synthesis, automating code review across unfamiliar repositories, and handling customer escalations that demand nuanced domain knowledge. \textbf{Each new task domain requires painstaking, expert-driven harness engineering}: designing the prompts, tools, orchestration logic, and evaluation criteria that make a foundation model effective. We present a two-level framework that automates this process. At the first level, the \textbf{Harness Evolution Loop} optimizes a worker agent's harness $\mathcal{H}$ for a single task: a Worker Agent $W_{\mathcal{H}}$ executes the task, an Evaluator Agent $V$ adversarially diagnoses failures and scores performance, and an Evolution Agent $E$ modifies the harness based on the full history of prior attempts. At the second level, the \textbf{Meta-Evolution Loop} optimizes the evolution protocol $Λ= (W_{\mathcal{H}}, \mathcal{H}^{(0)}, V, E)$ itself across diverse tasks, \textbf{learning a protocol $Λ^{(\text{best})}$ that enables rapid harness convergence on any new task -- so that adapting an agent to a novel domain requires no human harness engineering at all.} We formalize the correspondence to meta-learning and present both algorithms. The framework \textbf{shifts manual harness engineering into automated harness engineering}, and takes one step further -- \textbf{automating the design of the automation itself}.

</details>


### [75] [Deep FinResearch Bench: Evaluating AI's Ability to Conduct Professional Financial Investment Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21006)
*Mirazul Haque,Antony Papadimitriou,Samuel Mensah,Zhiqiang Ma,Zhijin Guo,Joy Prakash Sain,Simerjot Kaur,Charese Smiley,Xiaomo Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Deep FinResearch Bench，一个用于评估金融投资研究中深度研究（DR）智能体报告质量的综合框架。该基准从定性严谨性、定量预测与估值准确性、以及声明的可信度与可验证性三个维度进行评估，并设计了自动评分流程。实验发现AI生成报告在各维度上仍逊于金融专业人士撰写的报告，表明需要金融领域专精的DR智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度研究智能体评估缺乏针对金融投资研究领域的标准化基准，无法全面衡量报告在定性分析、定量预测和可验证性等方面的质量，因此需要一个专门面向金融领域的评估框架。

Method: 构建了Deep FinResearch Bench评估框架，从定性严谨性、定量预测与估值准确性、声明可信度与可验证性三个维度定义评价指标，并实现自动化评分流程。将该基准应用于前沿DR智能体生成的金融报告，并与金融专业人士撰写的报告进行对比。

Result: AI生成的金融报告在定性严谨性、定量预测与估值准确性、声明可信度与可验证性三个维度上均低于金融专业人士撰写的报告。

Conclusion: 当前前沿DR智能体在金融投资研究领域表现不足，需要开发面向金融领域的专用DR智能体；该工作为金融研究中DR智能体的标准化基准评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce Deep FinResearch Bench, a practical and comprehensive evaluation framework for deep research (DR) agents in financial investment research. The benchmark assesses three dimensions of report quality: qualitative rigor, quantitative forecasting and valuation accuracy, and claim credibility and verifiability. Particularly, we define corresponding qualitative and quantitative evaluation metrics and implement an automated scoring procedure to enable scalable assessment. Applying the benchmark to financial reports from frontier DR agents and comparing them with reports authored by financial professionals, we find that AI-generated reports still fall short across these dimensions. These findings underscore the need for domain-specialized DR agents tailored to finance, and we hope the work establishes a foundation for standardized benchmarking of DR agents in financial research.

</details>


### [76] [Adaptive Test-Time Compute Allocation with Evolving In-Context Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21018)
*Bowen Zuo,Dongruo Zhou,Yinglun Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应测试时计算分配框架，通过热身阶段识别简单查询和构建初始问答池，以及自适应阶段集中计算于未解决查询并通过动态上下文示例重塑生成分布，在数学、编码和推理基准上以更少推理计算超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时计算方法要么依赖静态计算分配，要么从固定生成分布中采样，缺乏对计算分配和生成过程的自适应调整能力。

Method: 两阶段框架：1）热身阶段，识别简单查询并从测试集自身构建初始问答池；2）自适应阶段，将额外计算集中于未解决查询，通过从语义相关查询的成功响应中提取动态上下文示例来重塑生成分布，而非从固定分布中重采样。

Result: 在数学、编码和推理基准上持续优于现有基线，同时显著减少推理时计算消耗。

Conclusion: 联合自适应计算分配和生成分布重塑是一种比静态分配或固定分布采样更高效的测试时计算策略。

Abstract: While scaling test-time compute can substantially improve model performance, existing approaches either rely on static compute allocation or sample from fixed generation distributions. In this work, we introduce a test-time compute allocation framework that jointly adapts where computation is spent and how generation is performed. Our method begins with a warm-up phase that identifies easy queries and assembles an initial pool of question-response pairs from the test set itself. An adaptive phase then concentrates further computation on unresolved queries while reshaping their generation distributions through evolving in-context demonstrations -- conditioning each generation on successful responses from semantically related queries rather than resampling from a fixed distribution. Experiments across math, coding, and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing baselines while consuming substantially less inference-time compute.

</details>


### [77] [HypEHR: Hyperbolic Modeling of Electronic Health Records for Efficient Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21027)
*Yuyu Liu,Sarang Rajendra Patil,Mengjia Xu,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HypEHR是一个紧凑的洛伦兹模型，利用双曲几何嵌入代码、就诊和问题，通过几何一致的交叉注意力和类型特定的指针头回答EHR问题，在MIMIC-IV基准上以远少于LLM的参数达到接近LLM的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的EHR问答管道部署成本高，且没有明确利用临床数据的层级结构。医学本体和患者轨迹具有双曲几何特性，因此作者提出利用双曲空间建模EHR问答任务。

Method: 提出HypEHR模型：1）在双曲空间（洛伦兹模型）中嵌入诊断代码、就诊记录和问题；2）通过几何一致的交叉注意力机制和类型特定的指针头回答查询；3）使用下次就诊诊断预测和层级感知的正则化进行预训练，以对齐ICD本体表征。

Result: 在两个MIMIC-IV的EHR问答基准上，HypEHR以远少于LLM的参数达到了接近LLM方法的性能。

Conclusion: 双曲几何建模能够有效捕获临床数据的层级结构，使紧凑模型在EHR问答任务上逼近大型LLM的性能，为低成本部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) question answering is often handled by LLM-based pipelines that are costly to deploy and do not explicitly leverage the hierarchical structure of clinical data. Motivated by evidence that medical ontologies and patient trajectories exhibit hyperbolic geometry, we propose HypEHR, a compact Lorentzian model that embeds codes, visits, and questions in hyperbolic space and answers queries via geometry-consistent cross-attention with type-specific pointer heads. HypEHR is pretrained with next-visit diagnosis prediction and hierarchy-aware regularization to align representations with the ICD ontology. On two MIMIC-IV-based EHR-QA benchmarks, HypEHR approaches LLM-based methods while using far fewer parameters. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yuyuliu11037/HypEHR.

</details>


### [78] [Who Defines Fairness? Target-Based Prompting for Demographic Representation in Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21036)
*Marzia Binta Nizam,James Davis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级、推理阶段的框架，通过提示层面干预来缓解文本到图像模型中的代表性偏见。用户可从多种公平性定义中选择（如均匀分布或LLM驱动的复杂定义），框架据此构建人口统计学特定的提示变体，在不修改底层模型的情况下实现可控制、透明的公平性干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型（如Stable Diffusion、DALL-E）在生成图像时复制社会偏见（如高地位职业倾向生成浅肤色图像），而现有缓解方法需要重新训练或精心策划的数据集，普通用户无法使用。

Method: 提出推理阶段的轻量级框架，用户从多种公平性规范中选择（均匀分布到LLM驱动的复杂定义），根据目标分布构建人口统计学特定的提示变体，通过审计检测生成结果与声明目标的对齐程度，并测量实际肤色分布而非假设均匀性为"公平"。

Result: 在涵盖30个职业和6个非职业上下文的36个提示上，该方法使生成的肤色分布朝向声明目标方向偏移，并在目标直接在肤色空间中定义时减少了与目标的偏差。

Conclusion: 该工作证明公平性干预可以在推理阶段实现透明、可控且可用的，直接赋能生成式AI用户，无需修改底层模型或依赖精心策划的数据集。

Abstract: Text-to-image(T2I) models like Stable Diffusion and DALL-E have made generative AI widely accessible, yet recent studies reveal that these systems often replicate societal biases, particularly in how they depict demographic groups across professions. Prompts such as 'doctor' or 'CEO' frequently yield lighter-skinned outputs, while lower-status roles like 'janitor' show more diversity, reinforcing stereotypes. Existing mitigation methods typically require retraining or curated datasets, making them inaccessible to most users. We propose a lightweight, inference-time framework that mitigates representational bias through prompt-level intervention without modifying the underlying model. Instead of assuming a single definition of fairness, our approach allows users to select among multiple fairness specifications-ranging from simple choices such as a uniform distribution to more complex definitions informed by a large language model(LLM) that cites sources and provides confidence estimates. These distributions guide the construction of demographic specific prompt variants in the corresponding proportions, and we evaluate alignment by auditing adherence to the declared target and measuring the resulting skin tone distribution rather than assuming uniformity as 'fairness'. Across 36 prompts spanning 30 occupations and 6 non-occupational contexts, our method shifts observed skin-tone outcomes in directions consistent with the declared target, and reduces deviation from targets when the target is defined directly in skin-tone space(fallback).
